1:import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
2:
3:import {
4:  composeConfirmationEmail,
5:  type ConfirmationEmailInput,
6:} from "./confirmation-email";
7:
8:const token = "a".repeat(64);
9:
10:const baseInput: ConfirmationEmailInput = {
11:  publicOrigin: "https://skillcima.com",
12:  firstName: "Amina",
13:  courseSlug: "forex-foundations",
14:  confirmationToken: token,
15:  confirmationExpiresAt: "2026-08-21T12:00:00.000Z",
16:};
17:
18:describe("Skillcima confirmation email composition", () => {
19:  it("builds the expected Skillcima confirmation URL and content", () => {
20:    const result = composeConfirmationEmail(baseInput);
21:
22:    expect(result.status).toBe("ready");
23:
24:    if (result.status !== "ready") {
25:      throw new Error("Expected ready confirmation email");
26:    }
27:
28:    expect(result.confirmationUrl).toBe(
29:      `https://skillcima.com/confirm?token=${token}`,
30:    );
31:
32:    expect(result.subject).toBe(
33:      "Confirm your free Skillcima Forex Foundations course",
34:    );
35:
36:    expect(result.text).toContain("free five-day Forex Foundations course");
37:
38:    expect(result.html).toContain("Confirm my course");
39:  });
40:
41:  it("uses the learner first name when available", () => {
42:    const result = composeConfirmationEmail(baseInput);
43:
44:    expect(result.status).toBe("ready");
45:
46:    if (result.status !== "ready") {
47:      throw new Error("Expected ready confirmation email");
48:    }
49:
50:    expect(result.text).toContain("Hi Amina,");
51:
52:    expect(result.html).toContain("Hi Amina,");
53:  });
54:
55:  it("uses a generic greeting when no first name is available", () => {
56:    const result = composeConfirmationEmail({
57:      ...baseInput,
58:      firstName: null,
59:    });
60:
61:    expect(result.status).toBe("ready");
62:
63:    if (result.status !== "ready") {
64:      throw new Error("Expected ready confirmation email");
65:    }
66:
67:    expect(result.text).toContain("Hello,");
68:
69:    expect(result.html).toContain("Hello,");
70:  });
71:
72:  it("escapes learner-controlled text in HTML", () => {
73:    const result = composeConfirmationEmail({
74:      ...baseInput,
75:      firstName: '<script>alert("x")</script>',
76:    });
77:
78:    expect(result.status).toBe("ready");
79:
80:    if (result.status !== "ready") {
81:      throw new Error("Expected ready confirmation email");
82:    }
83:
84:    expect(result.html).not.toContain("<script>");
85:
86:    expect(result.html).toContain("&lt;script&gt;");
87:  });
88:
89:  it("rejects malformed confirmation tokens", () => {
90:    expect(
91:      composeConfirmationEmail({
92:        ...baseInput,
93:        confirmationToken: "not-a-token",
94:      }),
95:    ).toEqual({
96:      status: "invalid_input",
97:    });
98:  });
99:
100:  it("rejects unsupported course slugs and invalid expiry values", () => {
101:    expect(
102:      composeConfirmationEmail({
103:        ...baseInput,
104:        courseSlug: "unknown-course",
105:      }),
106:    ).toEqual({
107:      status: "invalid_input",
108:    });
109:

111:      composeConfirmationEmail({

115:    ).toEqual({
116:      status: "invalid_input",

118:  });

120:  it("requires HTTPS except for explicit localhost development origins", () => {
121:    expect(
122:      composeConfirmationEmail({

126:    ).toEqual({

131:      composeConfirmationEmail({

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\confirmation-email.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:const TOKEN_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
2:
3:const COURSE_NAMES = {
4:  "forex-foundations": "Forex Foundations",
5:} as const;
6:
7:type SupportedCourseSlug = keyof typeof COURSE_NAMES;
8:
9:export interface ConfirmationEmailInput {
10:  publicOrigin: string;
11:  firstName: string | null;
12:  courseSlug: string;
13:  confirmationToken: string;
14:  confirmationExpiresAt: string;
15:}
16:
17:export type ConfirmationEmailContentResult =
18:  | {
19:      status: "ready";
20:      subject: string;
21:      text: string;
22:      html: string;
23:      confirmationUrl: string;
24:    }
25:  | {
26:      status: "invalid_input";
27:    };
28:
29:function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
30:  return value
31:    .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
32:    .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
33:    .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
34:    .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
35:    .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");
36:}
37:
38:function normalizeFirstName(value: string | null): string | null {
39:  if (value === null) {
40:    return null;
41:  }
42:
43:  const normalized = value.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
44:
45:  if (normalized.length < 1 || normalized.length > 100) {
46:    return null;
47:  }
48:
49:  return normalized;
50:}
51:
52:function parsePublicOrigin(value: string): URL | null {
53:  let url: URL;
54:
55:  try {
56:    url = new URL(value);
57:  } catch {
58:    return null;
59:  }
60:
61:  const isHttps = url.protocol === "https:";
62:
63:  const isLocalHttp =
64:    url.protocol === "http:" &&
65:    (url.hostname === "localhost" || url.hostname === "127.0.0.1");
66:
67:  if (!isHttps && !isLocalHttp) {
68:    return null;
69:  }
70:
71:  if (url.username || url.password) {
72:    return null;
73:  }
74:
75:  url.pathname = "/";
76:  url.search = "";
77:  url.hash = "";
78:
79:  return url;
80:}
81:
82:function isSupportedCourseSlug(value: string): value is SupportedCourseSlug {
83:  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(COURSE_NAMES, value);
84:}
85:
86:export function composeConfirmationEmail(
87:  input: ConfirmationEmailInput,
88:): ConfirmationEmailContentResult {
89:  const origin = parsePublicOrigin(input.publicOrigin);
90:
91:  if (!origin) {
92:    return {
93:      status: "invalid_input",
94:    };
95:  }
96:
97:  if (!TOKEN_PATTERN.test(input.confirmationToken)) {
98:    return {
99:      status: "invalid_input",
100:    };
101:  }
102:
103:  if (!isSupportedCourseSlug(input.courseSlug)) {
104:    return {
105:      status: "invalid_input",
106:    };
107:  }
108:
109:  const expiresAt = Date.parse(input.confirmationExpiresAt);
110:
111:  if (!Number.isFinite(expiresAt)) {
112:    return {
113:      status: "invalid_input",
114:    };
115:  }
116:
117:  const firstName = normalizeFirstName(input.firstName);
118:
119:  if (input.firstName !== null && firstName === null) {
120:    return {
121:      status: "invalid_input",
122:    };
123:  }
124:
125:  const courseName = COURSE_NAMES[input.courseSlug];
126:
127:  const confirmationUrl = new URL("/confirm", origin);
128:
129:  confirmationUrl.searchParams.set("token", input.confirmationToken);
130:
131:  const url = confirmationUrl.toString();
132:
133:  const greeting = firstName ? `Hi ${firstName},` : "Hello,";
134:
135:  const subject = `Confirm your free Skillcima ${courseName} course`;
136:
137:  const text = [
138:    greeting,
139:    "",
140:    `You asked to receive Skillcima's free five-day ${courseName} course by email.`,
141:    "",
142:    "Confirm your course:",
143:    url,
144:    "",
145:    "For security, this confirmation link expires automatically.",
146:    "",
147:    "If you did not request this course, you can ignore this email.",
148:    "",
149:    "Skillcima",
150:  ].join("\n");
151:
152:  const escapedGreeting = escapeHtml(greeting);
153:
154:  const escapedCourseName = escapeHtml(courseName);
155:
156:  const escapedUrl = escapeHtml(url);
157:
158:  const html = [
159:    "<!doctype html>",
160:    '<html lang="en">',
161:    "<head>",
162:    '<meta charset="utf-8">',
163:    '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">',
164:    "<title>Confirm your Skillcima course</title>",
165:    "</head>",
166:    '<body style="margin:0;padding:0;background:#f7f5ed;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#111827;">',
167:    '<div style="max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;padding:32px 20px;">',
168:    '<div style="background:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;padding:32px;">',
169:    '<p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:16px;">',
170:    escapedGreeting,
171:    "</p>",
172:    '<h1 style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:28px;line-height:1.2;">Confirm your free course</h1>',
173:    '<p style="margin:0 0 24px;font-size:16px;line-height:1.6;">',
174:    `You asked to receive Skillcima's free five-day ${escapedCourseName} course by email.`,
175:    "</p>",
176:    '<p style="margin:0 0 24px;">',
177:    `<a href="${escapedUrl}" style="display:inline-block;padding:14px 22px;border-radius:10px;background:#111827;color:#ffffff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:700;">Confirm my course</a>`,
178:    "</p>",
179:    '<p style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6;color:#4b5563;">For security, this confirmation link expires automatically.</p>',
180:    '<p style="margin:0;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6;color:#4b5563;">If you did not request this course, you can ignore this email.</p>',
181:    "</div>",
182:    '<p style="margin:20px 0 0;text-align:center;font-size:13px;color:#6b7280;">Skillcima</p>',
183:    "</div>",
184:    "</body>",
185:    "</html>",
186:  ].join("");
187:
188:  return {
189:    status: "ready",
190:    subject,
191:    text,
192:    html,
193:    confirmationUrl: url,
194:  };
195:}

1:import {
2:  composeConfirmationEmail,
3:  type ConfirmationEmailContentResult,
4:} from "./confirmation-email";
5:import {
6:  prepareConfirmationDelivery,
7:  type ConfirmationDeliveryEnv,
8:  type PrepareConfirmationDeliveryResult,
9:} from "./confirmation-delivery";
10:import {
11:  sendEmailWithResend,
12:  type ResendEmailEnv,
13:  type ResendEmailResult,
14:} from "./resend-email";
15:import type {
16:  EmailDeliveryAdapter,
17:  EmailDeliveryInput,
18:  EmailDeliveryResult,
19:} from "./email-queue-processor";
20:
21:export type ConfirmationEmailDeliveryEnv = ConfirmationDeliveryEnv &
22:  ResendEmailEnv & {
23:    SKILLCIMA_PUBLIC_ORIGIN: string;
24:  };
25:
26:export interface ConfirmationEmailDeliveryDependencies {
27:  prepareConfirmationDelivery: typeof prepareConfirmationDelivery;
28:
29:  composeConfirmationEmail: typeof composeConfirmationEmail;
30:
31:  sendEmailWithResend: typeof sendEmailWithResend;
32:}
33:
34:const defaultDependencies: ConfirmationEmailDeliveryDependencies = {
35:  prepareConfirmationDelivery,
36:  composeConfirmationEmail,
37:  sendEmailWithResend,
38:};
39:
40:function temporaryFailure(errorCode: string): EmailDeliveryResult {
41:  return {
42:    status: "temporary_failure",
43:    errorCode,
44:  };
45:}
46:
47:function permanentFailure(errorCode: string): EmailDeliveryResult {
48:  return {
49:    status: "permanent_failure",
50:    errorCode,
51:  };
52:}
53:
54:function mapPreparationFailure(
55:  result: Exclude<PrepareConfirmationDeliveryResult, { status: "ready" }>,
56:): EmailDeliveryResult {
57:  switch (result.status) {
58:    case "misconfigured":
59:      return temporaryFailure("CONFIRMATION_DELIVERY_NOT_CONFIGURED");
60:
61:    case "unavailable":
62:      return temporaryFailure("CONFIRMATION_DELIVERY_UNAVAILABLE");
63:
64:    case "invalid_job_state":
65:      /*
66:       * A successful Queue claim followed by an
67:       * invalid database state can be caused by a
68:       * concurrent state transition. Retry rather
69:       * than terminally discarding the job.
70:       */
71:      return temporaryFailure("CONFIRMATION_JOB_STATE_RACE");
72:
73:    case "invalid_job_id":
74:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_JOB_INVALID");
75:
76:    case "not_found":
77:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_JOB_NOT_FOUND");
78:
79:    case "invalid_job_type":
80:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_JOB_TYPE_INVALID");
81:
82:    case "already_confirmed":
83:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_ALREADY_CONFIRMED");
84:
85:    case "not_deliverable":
86:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_NOT_DELIVERABLE");
87:
88:    case "invalid_enrolment_state":
89:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_ENROLMENT_INVALID");
90:
91:    case "expired":
92:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_LINK_EXPIRED");
93:
94:    case "token_mismatch":
95:      return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_TOKEN_MISMATCH");
96:  }
97:}
98:
99:function mapProviderResult(result: ResendEmailResult): EmailDeliveryResult {
100:  if (result.status === "accepted") {
101:    return {
102:      status: "accepted",
103:      providerMessageId: result.providerMessageId,
104:    };
105:  }
106:
107:  if (result.status === "misconfigured") {
108:    return temporaryFailure("RESEND_NOT_CONFIGURED");
109:  }
110:
111:  if (result.status === "invalid_input") {
112:    return permanentFailure("RESEND_INVALID_INPUT");
113:  }
114:
115:  return {
116:    status: result.status,
117:    errorCode: result.errorCode,
118:  };
119:}
120:
121:export function createConfirmationEmailDelivery(
122:  env: ConfirmationEmailDeliveryEnv,
123:  dependencies: ConfirmationEmailDeliveryDependencies = defaultDependencies,
124:): EmailDeliveryAdapter {
125:  return {
126:    async deliver(input: EmailDeliveryInput): Promise<EmailDeliveryResult> {
127:      if (input.jobType !== "course_confirmation") {
128:        return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_JOB_TYPE_INVALID");
129:      }
130:
131:      let prepared: PrepareConfirmationDeliveryResult;
132:
133:      try {
134:        prepared = await dependencies.prepareConfirmationDelivery(
135:          env,
136:          input.jobId,
137:        );
138:      } catch {
139:        return temporaryFailure("CONFIRMATION_PREPARATION_EXCEPTION");
140:      }
141:
142:      if (prepared.status !== "ready") {
143:        return mapPreparationFailure(prepared);
144:      }
145:
146:      let content: ConfirmationEmailContentResult;
147:
148:      try {
149:        content = dependencies.composeConfirmationEmail({
150:          publicOrigin: env.SKILLCIMA_PUBLIC_ORIGIN,
151:          firstName: prepared.firstName,
152:          courseSlug: prepared.courseSlug,
153:          confirmationToken: prepared.confirmationToken,
154:          confirmationExpiresAt: prepared.confirmationExpiresAt,
155:        });
156:      } catch {
157:        return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_EMAIL_COMPOSITION_EXCEPTION");
158:      }
159:
160:      if (content.status !== "ready") {
161:        return permanentFailure("CONFIRMATION_EMAIL_INVALID");
162:      }
163:
164:      let provider: ResendEmailResult;
165:
166:      try {
167:        provider = await dependencies.sendEmailWithResend(env, {
168:          to: prepared.recipientEmail,
169:          subject: content.subject,
170:          html: content.html,
171:          text: content.text,
172:          idempotencyKey: input.idempotencyKey,
173:        });
174:      } catch {
175:        return temporaryFailure("RESEND_DELIVERY_EXCEPTION");
176:      }
177:
178:      return mapProviderResult(provider);
179:    },
180:  };
181:}

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\confirmation-token.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:const UUID_PATTERN =
2:  /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
3:
4:const CONFIRMATION_TOKEN_NAMESPACE = "skillcima-confirmation:v1";
5:
6:const MINIMUM_SECRET_BYTES = 32;
7:
8:export interface ConfirmationTokenEnv {
9:  SKILLCIMA_CONFIRMATION_TOKEN_SECRET: string;
10:}
11:
12:export type ConfirmationTokenResult =
13:  | {
14:      status: "ready";
15:      token: string;
16:      tokenHash: string;
17:    }
18:  | {
19:      status: "misconfigured";
20:    }
21:  | {
22:      status: "invalid_job_id";
23:    };
24:
25:function bytesToHex(bytes: ArrayBuffer): string {
26:  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(bytes))
27:    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
28:    .join("");
29:}
30:
31:async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {
32:  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(value);
33:
34:  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes);
35:
36:  return bytesToHex(digest);
37:}
38:
39:export async function deriveConfirmationToken(
40:  env: ConfirmationTokenEnv,
41:  jobId: string,
42:): Promise<ConfirmationTokenResult> {
43:  if (!UUID_PATTERN.test(jobId)) {
44:    return {
45:      status: "invalid_job_id",
46:    };
47:  }
48:
49:  const secret = env.SKILLCIMA_CONFIRMATION_TOKEN_SECRET?.trim();
50:
51:  if (!secret) {
52:    return {
53:      status: "misconfigured",
54:    };
55:  }
56:
57:  const secretBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(secret);
58:
59:  if (secretBytes.byteLength < MINIMUM_SECRET_BYTES) {
60:    return {
61:      status: "misconfigured",
62:    };
63:  }
64:
65:  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
66:    "raw",
67:    secretBytes,
68:    {
69:      name: "HMAC",
70:      hash: "SHA-256",
71:    },
72:    false,
73:    ["sign"],
74:  );
75:
76:  const message = `${CONFIRMATION_TOKEN_NAMESPACE}:${jobId}`;
77:
78:  const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign(
79:    "HMAC",
80:    key,
81:    new TextEncoder().encode(message),
82:  );
83:
84:  const token = bytesToHex(signature);
85:  const tokenHash = await sha256Hex(token);
86:
87:  return {
88:    status: "ready",
89:    token,
90:    tokenHash,
91:  };
92:}

9:  "Submitting the Skillcima enrolment form requests delivery of the free five-day Forex Foundations course by email.";

12:  "Send me continuing educational emails.";

26:      category: "course_delivery",
27:      action: "requested",
28:      privacyNoticeVersion: SKILLCIMA_PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION,
29:      consentWording: COURSE_DELIVERY_WORDING,

37:      category: "educational_newsletter",
38:      action: "granted",

40:      consentWording: NEWSLETTER_CONSENT_WORDING,

42:      landingPageVersion: SKILLCIMA_LANDING_PAGE_VERSION,
43:    });
44:  }

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\course-confirmation.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:import type { SupabaseEnv } from "./supabase";
2:
3:const TOKEN_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
4:
5:const UUID_PATTERN =
6:  /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
7:
8:type ConfirmationDatabaseStatus =
9:  | "confirmed"
10:  | "already_confirmed"
11:  | "expired"
12:  | "not_found"
13:  | "not_deliverable"
14:  | "invalid_enrolment_state";
15:
16:interface ConfirmationRpcRow {
17:  result_status: ConfirmationDatabaseStatus;
18:
19:  result_enrolment_id: string | null;
20:
21:  result_course_slug: string | null;
22:
23:  result_confirmed_at: string | null;
24:}
25:
26:interface SupabaseConfiguration {
27:  url: string;
28:  secretKey: string;
29:}
30:
31:export type ConfirmCourseResult =
32:  | {
33:      status: "confirmed" | "already_confirmed";
34:
35:      enrolmentId: string;
36:      courseSlug: string;
37:      confirmedAt: string;
38:    }
39:  | {
40:      status: "expired" | "not_deliverable" | "invalid_enrolment_state";
41:
42:      enrolmentId: string;
43:      courseSlug: string;
44:    }
45:  | {
46:      status: "not_found";
47:    }
48:  | {
49:      status: "invalid_token";
50:    }
51:  | {
52:      status: "misconfigured";
53:    }
54:  | {
55:      status: "unavailable";
56:      httpStatus?: number;
57:    };
58:
59:function getSupabaseConfiguration(
60:  env: SupabaseEnv,
61:): SupabaseConfiguration | null {
62:  const url = env.SUPABASE_URL?.trim();
63:
64:  const secretKey = env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY?.trim();
65:
66:  if (!url || !secretKey) {
67:    return null;
68:  }
69:
70:  return {
71:    url: url.replace(/\/+$/, ""),
72:    secretKey,
73:  };
74:}
75:
76:function bytesToHex(bytes: ArrayBuffer): string {
77:  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(bytes))
78:    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
79:    .join("");
80:}
81:
82:async function hashConfirmationToken(token: string): Promise<string> {
83:  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(token);
84:
85:  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes);
86:
87:  return bytesToHex(digest);
88:}
89:
90:function isDatabaseStatus(value: unknown): value is ConfirmationDatabaseStatus {
91:  return (
92:    value === "confirmed" ||
93:    value === "already_confirmed" ||
94:    value === "expired" ||
95:    value === "not_found" ||
96:    value === "not_deliverable" ||
97:    value === "invalid_enrolment_state"
98:  );
99:}
100:
101:function isValidCourseSlug(value: unknown): value is string {
102:  return typeof value === "string" && value.length >= 1 && value.length <= 100;
103:}
104:
105:function isValidTimestamp(value: unknown): value is string {
106:  return typeof value === "string" && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));
107:}
108:
109:export async function confirmCourse(
110:  env: SupabaseEnv,
111:  rawToken: string,
112:): Promise<ConfirmCourseResult> {
113:  if (typeof rawToken !== "string" || !TOKEN_PATTERN.test(rawToken)) {
114:    return {
115:      status: "invalid_token",
116:    };
117:  }
118:
119:  const configuration = getSupabaseConfiguration(env);
120:
121:  if (!configuration) {
122:    return {
123:      status: "misconfigured",
124:    };
125:  }
126:
127:  const tokenHash = await hashConfirmationToken(rawToken);
128:
129:  const url = new URL(
130:    `${configuration.url}/rest/v1/rpc/skillcima_confirm_course`,
131:  );
132:
133:  let response: Response;
134:
135:  try {
136:    response = await fetch(url, {
137:      method: "POST",
138:      headers: {
139:        Accept: "application/json",
140:        "Content-Type": "application/json",
141:        apikey: configuration.secretKey,
142:      },
143:      body: JSON.stringify({
144:        p_confirmation_token_hash: tokenHash,
145:      }),
146:    });
147:  } catch {
148:    return {
149:      status: "unavailable",
150:    };
151:  }
152:
153:  if (!response.ok) {
154:    const httpStatus = response.status;
155:
156:    await response.body?.cancel();
157:
158:    return {
159:      status: "unavailable",
160:      httpStatus,
161:    };
162:  }
163:
164:  let body: unknown;
165:
166:  try {
167:    body = (await response.json()) as unknown;
168:  } catch {
169:    return {
170:      status: "unavailable",
171:      httpStatus: response.status,
172:    };
173:  }
174:
175:  if (!Array.isArray(body) || body.length !== 1) {
176:    return {
177:      status: "unavailable",
178:    };
179:  }
180:
181:  const row = body[0];
182:
183:  if (!row || typeof row !== "object" || Array.isArray(row)) {
184:    return {
185:      status: "unavailable",
186:    };
187:  }
188:
189:  const candidate = row as Partial<ConfirmationRpcRow>;
190:
191:  if (!isDatabaseStatus(candidate.result_status)) {
192:    return {
193:      status: "unavailable",
194:    };
195:  }
196:
197:  if (candidate.result_status === "not_found") {
198:    return {
199:      status: "not_found",
200:    };
201:  }
202:
203:  if (
204:    typeof candidate.result_enrolment_id !== "string" ||
205:    !UUID_PATTERN.test(candidate.result_enrolment_id) ||
206:    !isValidCourseSlug(candidate.result_course_slug)
207:  ) {
208:    return {
209:      status: "unavailable",
210:    };
211:  }
212:
213:  if (
214:    candidate.result_status === "confirmed" ||
215:    candidate.result_status === "already_confirmed"
216:  ) {
217:    if (!isValidTimestamp(candidate.result_confirmed_at)) {
218:      return {
219:        status: "unavailable",
220:      };
221:    }
222:
223:    return {
224:      status: candidate.result_status,
225:
226:      enrolmentId: candidate.result_enrolment_id,
227:
228:      courseSlug: candidate.result_course_slug,
229:
230:      confirmedAt: candidate.result_confirmed_at,
231:    };
232:  }
233:
234:  return {
235:    status: candidate.result_status,
236:
237:    enrolmentId: candidate.result_enrolment_id,
238:
239:    courseSlug: candidate.result_course_slug,
240:  };
241:}

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\course-confirmation-route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:import { confirmCourse, type ConfirmCourseResult } from "./course-confirmation";
2:
3:import type { SupabaseEnv } from "./supabase";
4:
5:const MAX_CONFIRMATION_BODY_BYTES = 2048;
6:
7:export interface CourseConfirmationRouteDependencies {
8:  confirmCourse: typeof confirmCourse;
9:}
10:
11:const defaultDependencies: CourseConfirmationRouteDependencies = {
12:  confirmCourse,
13:};
14:
15:interface ConfirmationRequestBody {
16:  token: string;
17:}
18:
19:function jsonHeaders(): Headers {
20:  return new Headers({
21:    "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
22:    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
23:    "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
24:    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
25:  });
26:}
27:
28:function jsonResponse(status: number, body: unknown): Response {
29:  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
30:    status,
31:    headers: jsonHeaders(),
32:  });
33:}
34:
35:function isJsonContentType(request: Request): boolean {
36:  const value = request.headers.get("Content-Type")?.toLowerCase();
37:
38:  if (!value) {
39:    return false;
40:  }
41:
42:  return value === "application/json" || value.startsWith("application/json;");
43:}
44:
45:function isExactConfirmationBody(
46:  value: unknown,
47:): value is ConfirmationRequestBody {
48:  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
49:    return false;
50:  }
51:
52:  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
53:
54:  const keys = Object.keys(record);
55:
56:  return (
57:    keys.length === 1 && keys[0] === "token" && typeof record.token === "string"
58:  );
59:}
60:
61:function mapConfirmationResult(result: ConfirmCourseResult): Response {
62:  switch (result.status) {
63:    case "confirmed":
64:      return jsonResponse(200, {
65:        ok: true,
66:        status: "confirmed",
67:        courseSlug: result.courseSlug,
68:      });
69:
70:    case "already_confirmed":
71:      return jsonResponse(200, {
72:        ok: true,
73:        status: "already_confirmed",
74:        courseSlug: result.courseSlug,
75:      });
76:
77:    case "expired":
78:      return jsonResponse(410, {
79:        ok: false,
80:        error: {
81:          code: "CONFIRMATION_EXPIRED",
82:          message: "This confirmation link has expired.",
83:        },
84:      });
85:
86:    case "invalid_token":
87:    case "not_found":
88:      return jsonResponse(400, {
89:        ok: false,
90:        error: {
91:          code: "INVALID_CONFIRMATION_LINK",
92:          message: "This confirmation link is invalid.",
93:        },
94:      });
95:
96:    case "not_deliverable":
97:      return jsonResponse(409, {
98:        ok: false,
99:        error: {
100:          code: "CONFIRMATION_NOT_AVAILABLE",
101:          message: "This course enrolment cannot be confirmed.",
102:        },
103:      });
104:
105:    case "invalid_enrolment_state":
106:      return jsonResponse(409, {
107:        ok: false,
108:        error: {
109:          code: "CONFIRMATION_STATE_INVALID",
110:          message: "This course enrolment cannot be confirmed.",
111:        },
112:      });
113:
114:    case "misconfigured":
115:      return jsonResponse(503, {
116:        ok: false,
117:        error: {
118:          code: "CONFIRMATION_NOT_CONFIGURED",
119:          message: "Confirmation is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
120:        },
121:      });
122:
123:    case "unavailable":
124:      return jsonResponse(503, {
125:        ok: false,
126:        error: {
127:          code: "CONFIRMATION_UNAVAILABLE",
128:          message: "Confirmation is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
129:        },
130:      });
131:  }
132:}
133:
134:export async function handleCourseConfirmationRequest(
135:  request: Request,
136:  env: SupabaseEnv,
137:  dependencies: CourseConfirmationRouteDependencies = defaultDependencies,
138:): Promise<Response> {
139:  if (request.method !== "POST") {
140:    return jsonResponse(405, {
141:      ok: false,
142:      error: {
143:        code: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED",
144:        message: "This endpoint only accepts POST requests.",
145:      },
146:    });
147:  }
148:
149:  if (!isJsonContentType(request)) {
150:    return jsonResponse(415, {
151:      ok: false,
152:      error: {
153:        code: "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE",
154:        message: "Content-Type must be application/json.",
155:      },
156:    });
157:  }
158:
159:  let rawBody: string;
160:
161:  try {
162:    rawBody = await request.text();
163:  } catch {
164:    return jsonResponse(400, {
165:      ok: false,
166:      error: {
167:        code: "INVALID_REQUEST_BODY",
168:        message: "The request body could not be read.",
169:      },
170:    });
171:  }
172:
173:  if (
174:    new TextEncoder().encode(rawBody).byteLength > MAX_CONFIRMATION_BODY_BYTES
175:  ) {
176:    return jsonResponse(413, {
177:      ok: false,
178:      error: {
179:        code: "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE",
180:        message: "The request body is too large.",
181:      },
182:    });
183:  }
184:
185:  if (!rawBody.trim()) {
186:    return jsonResponse(400, {
187:      ok: false,
188:      error: {
189:        code: "INVALID_REQUEST_BODY",
190:        message: "A JSON request body is required.",
191:      },
192:    });
193:  }
194:
195:  let parsed: unknown;
196:
197:  try {
198:    parsed = JSON.parse(rawBody);
199:  } catch {
200:    return jsonResponse(400, {
201:      ok: false,
202:      error: {
203:        code: "INVALID_JSON",
204:        message: "The request body contains invalid JSON.",
205:      },
206:    });
207:  }
208:
209:  if (!isExactConfirmationBody(parsed)) {
210:    return jsonResponse(400, {
211:      ok: false,
212:      error: {
213:        code: "INVALID_CONFIRMATION_REQUEST",
214:        message: "The confirmation request is invalid.",
215:      },
216:    });
217:  }
218:
219:  let result: ConfirmCourseResult;
220:
221:  try {
222:    result = await dependencies.confirmCourse(env, parsed.token);
223:  } catch {
224:    return jsonResponse(503, {
225:      ok: false,
226:      error: {
227:        code: "CONFIRMATION_UNAVAILABLE",
228:        message: "Confirmation is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
229:      },
230:    });
231:  }
232:
233:  return mapConfirmationResult(result);
234:}

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\course-lesson-email.ts

1:import {
2:  forexFoundationsEmailCourse,
3:  type FiveDayEmailJobType,
4:  type FiveDayEmailLesson,
5:} from "@repo/content";
6:
7:import { renderSkillcimaEmailLayout } from "./skillcima-email-layout";
8:import { skillcimaEmailTheme } from "./skillcima-email-theme";
9:
10:export interface ComposeCourseLessonEmailInput {
11:  courseSlug: string;
12:  jobType: FiveDayEmailJobType;
13:  firstName: string | null;
14:
15:  micrositeBaseUrl: string;
16:  mainSiteBaseUrl: string;
17:}
18:
19:export interface ComposedCourseLessonEmail {
20:  subject: string;
21:  html: string;
22:  text: string;
23:  contentVersion: string;
24:}
25:
26:function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
27:  return value
28:    .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
29:    .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
30:    .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
31:    .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
32:    .replaceAll("'", "&#39;");
33:}
34:
35:function requireHttpsOrigin(value: string, field: string): URL {
36:  const url = new URL(value);
37:
38:  if (
39:    url.protocol !== "https:" ||
40:    url.username ||
41:    url.password ||
42:    url.pathname !== "/" ||
43:    url.search ||
44:    url.hash
45:  ) {
46:    throw new Error(`COURSE_EMAIL_${field.toUpperCase()}_INVALID`);
47:  }
48:
49:  return url;
50:}
51:
52:function resolveCtaUrl(
53:  lesson: FiveDayEmailLesson,
54:  micrositeBaseUrl: URL,
55:  mainSiteBaseUrl: URL,
56:): string | null {
57:  if (lesson.cta.destination === "none" || lesson.cta.path === null) {
58:    return null;
59:  }
60:
61:  const baseUrl =
62:    lesson.cta.destination === "main_course_start"
63:      ? mainSiteBaseUrl
64:      : micrositeBaseUrl;
65:
66:  return new URL(lesson.cta.path, baseUrl).toString();
67:}
68:
69:export function composeCourseLessonEmail(
70:  input: ComposeCourseLessonEmailInput,
71:): ComposedCourseLessonEmail {
72:  if (input.courseSlug !== forexFoundationsEmailCourse.courseSlug) {
73:    throw new Error("COURSE_EMAIL_COURSE_UNSUPPORTED");
74:  }
75:
76:  const lesson = forexFoundationsEmailCourse.lessons.find(
77:    (candidate) => candidate.jobType === input.jobType,
78:  );
79:
80:  if (!lesson) {
81:    throw new Error("COURSE_EMAIL_LESSON_NOT_FOUND");
82:  }
83:
84:  const micrositeBaseUrl = requireHttpsOrigin(
85:    input.micrositeBaseUrl,
86:    "microsite_base_url",
87:  );
88:
89:  const mainSiteBaseUrl = requireHttpsOrigin(
90:    input.mainSiteBaseUrl,
91:    "main_site_base_url",
92:  );
93:
94:  const firstName = input.firstName?.trim() || null;
95:
96:  const greeting = firstName ? `Hi ${escapeHtml(firstName)},` : "Hi,";
97:
98:  const paragraphs = lesson.paragraphs
99:    .map(
100:      (paragraph) => `<p style="margin:0 0 18px;">${escapeHtml(paragraph)}</p>`,
101:    )
102:    .join("");
103:
104:  const points = lesson.keyPoints
105:    .map((point) => `<li style="margin:0 0 10px;">${escapeHtml(point)}</li>`)
106:    .join("");
107:
108:  const ctaUrl = resolveCtaUrl(lesson, micrositeBaseUrl, mainSiteBaseUrl);
109:
110:  const ctaHtml = ctaUrl
111:    ? `<p style="margin:28px 0 8px;">
112:        <a
113:          href="${escapeHtml(ctaUrl)}"
114:          style="display:inline-block;background:${skillcimaEmailTheme.colors.primary};color:${skillcimaEmailTheme.colors.text};text-decoration:none;font-weight:700;padding:14px 20px;border-radius:10px;"
115:        >${escapeHtml(lesson.cta.label)}</a>
116:      </p>`
117:    : "";
118:
119:  const trustedContentHtml = `
120:    <p style="margin:0 0 20px;">${greeting}</p>
121:
122:    <p style="margin:0 0 18px;">
123:      ${escapeHtml(lesson.intro)}
124:    </p>
125:
126:    ${paragraphs}
127:
128:    <div
129:      style="margin:28px 0;padding:20px;background:${skillcimaEmailTheme.colors.soft};border:1px solid ${skillcimaEmailTheme.colors.border};border-radius:12px;"
130:    >
131:      <strong>Key points</strong>
132:      <ul style="margin:14px 0 0;padding-left:22px;">
133:        ${points}
134:      </ul>
135:    </div>
136:
137:    <div
138:      style="margin:28px 0;padding:18px;border-left:4px solid ${skillcimaEmailTheme.colors.accent};background:${skillcimaEmailTheme.colors.background};"
139:    >
140:      <strong>Risk reminder</strong>
141:      <div style="margin-top:8px;">
142:        ${escapeHtml(lesson.riskNote)}
143:      </div>
144:    </div>
145:
146:    ${ctaHtml}
147:
148:    <p style="margin:28px 0 0;">
149:      ${escapeHtml(lesson.closing)}
150:    </p>
151:  `;
152:
153:  const html = renderSkillcimaEmailLayout({
154:    previewText: lesson.previewText,
155:    eyebrow: `Day ${lesson.day} · ${forexFoundationsEmailCourse.courseName}`,
156:    heading: lesson.heading,
157:    trustedContentHtml,
158:  });
159:
160:  const textParts = [
161:    firstName ? `Hi ${firstName},` : "Hi,",
162:    "",
163:    lesson.heading,
164:    "",
165:    lesson.intro,
166:    "",
167:    ...lesson.paragraphs,
168:    "",
169:    "Key points:",
170:    ...lesson.keyPoints.map((point) => `- ${point}`),
171:    "",
172:    `Risk reminder: ${lesson.riskNote}`,
173:  ];
174:
175:  if (ctaUrl) {
176:    textParts.push("", `${lesson.cta.label}: ${ctaUrl}`);
177:  }
178:
179:  textParts.push(
180:    "",
181:    lesson.closing,
182:    "",
183:    `${skillcimaEmailTheme.brandName} · ${skillcimaEmailTheme.domain}`,
184:  );
185:
186:  return {
187:    subject: lesson.subject,
188:    html,
189:    text: textParts.join("\n"),
190:    contentVersion: forexFoundationsEmailCourse.contentVersion,
191:  };
192:}

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\course-lesson-email-delivery.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:import {
2:  forexFoundationsEmailCourse,
3:  type FiveDayEmailJobType,
4:} from "@repo/content";
5:
6:import {
7:  composeCourseLessonEmail,
8:  type ComposedCourseLessonEmail,
9:} from "./course-lesson-email";
10:import {
11:  prepareCourseLessonDelivery,
12:  type PrepareCourseLessonDeliveryResult,
13:} from "./course-lesson-delivery";
14:import type { SkillcimaEmailQueueJobType } from "./email-queue";
15:import type {
16:  EmailDeliveryAdapter,
17:  EmailDeliveryInput,
18:  EmailDeliveryResult,
19:} from "./email-queue-processor";
20:import {
21:  sendEmailWithResend,
22:  type ResendEmailEnv,
23:  type ResendEmailInput,
24:  type ResendEmailResult,
25:} from "./resend-email";
26:import type { SupabaseEnv } from "./supabase";
27:
28:const SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_ORIGIN = "https://skillcima.com/";
29:
30:const PIPSTART_EMAIL_ORIGIN = "https://pipstart.net/";
31:
32:export type CourseLessonEmailDeliveryEnv = SupabaseEnv &
33:  Partial<ResendEmailEnv>;
34:
35:export type CourseLessonContentAvailability =
36:  "draft" | "approved" | "unsupported";
37:
38:type CourseLessonProviderSender = (
39:  env: CourseLessonEmailDeliveryEnv,
40:  input: ResendEmailInput,
41:) => Promise<ResendEmailResult>;
42:
43:export interface CourseLessonEmailDeliveryDependencies {
44:  prepareCourseLessonDelivery: typeof prepareCourseLessonDelivery;
45:
46:  getCourseLessonContentStatus?: (
47:    courseSlug: string,
48:  ) => CourseLessonContentAvailability;
49:
50:  composeCourseLessonEmail?: typeof composeCourseLessonEmail;
51:
52:  sendEmailWithResend?: CourseLessonProviderSender;
53:
54:  /*
55:   * M4F.2E.2 provider lock.
56:   *
57:   * Delivery remains disabled unless a future
58:   * controlled runtime step explicitly enables it.
59:   */
60:  enableProviderDelivery?: boolean;
61:}
62:
63:function getDefaultCourseLessonContentStatus(
64:  courseSlug: string,
65:): CourseLessonContentAvailability {
66:  if (courseSlug !== forexFoundationsEmailCourse.courseSlug) {
67:    return "unsupported";
68:  }
69:
70:  return forexFoundationsEmailCourse.status;
71:}
72:
73:async function sendCourseLessonWithResend(
74:  env: CourseLessonEmailDeliveryEnv,
75:  input: ResendEmailInput,
76:): Promise<ResendEmailResult> {
77:  return sendEmailWithResend(
78:    {
79:      RESEND_API_KEY: env.RESEND_API_KEY ?? "",
80:      SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_FROM: env.SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_FROM ?? "",
81:    },
82:    input,
83:  );
84:}
85:
86:const defaultDependencies: CourseLessonEmailDeliveryDependencies = {
87:  prepareCourseLessonDelivery,
88:  getCourseLessonContentStatus: getDefaultCourseLessonContentStatus,
89:  composeCourseLessonEmail,
90:  sendEmailWithResend: sendCourseLessonWithResend,
91:  enableProviderDelivery: false,
92:};
93:
94:function temporaryFailure(errorCode: string): EmailDeliveryResult {
95:  return {
96:    status: "temporary_failure",
97:    errorCode,
98:  };
99:}
100:
101:function permanentFailure(errorCode: string): EmailDeliveryResult {
102:  return {
103:    status: "permanent_failure",
104:    errorCode,
105:  };
106:}
107:
108:function isCourseLessonJobType(
109:  value: SkillcimaEmailQueueJobType,
110:): value is FiveDayEmailJobType {
111:  return (
112:    value === "course_day_1" ||
113:    value === "course_day_2" ||
114:    value === "course_day_3" ||
115:    value === "course_day_4" ||
116:    value === "course_day_5"
117:  );
118:}
119:
120:function mapPreparationFailure(
121:  result: Exclude<PrepareCourseLessonDeliveryResult, { status: "ready" }>,
122:): EmailDeliveryResult {
123:  switch (result.status) {
124:    case "misconfigured":
125:      return temporaryFailure("COURSE_LESSON_DELIVERY_NOT_CONFIGURED");
126:
127:    case "unavailable":
128:      return temporaryFailure("COURSE_LESSON_DELIVERY_UNAVAILABLE");
129:
130:    case "invalid_job_state":
131:      return temporaryFailure("COURSE_LESSON_JOB_STATE_RACE");
132:
133:    case "invalid_job_id":
134:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_JOB_INVALID");
135:
136:    case "not_found":
137:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_JOB_NOT_FOUND");
138:
139:    case "invalid_job_type":
140:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_JOB_TYPE_INVALID");
141:
142:    case "not_deliverable":
143:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_NOT_DELIVERABLE");
144:
145:    case "not_confirmed":
146:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_NOT_CONFIRMED");
147:
148:    case "invalid_enrolment_state":
149:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_ENROLMENT_INVALID");
150:
151:    case "invalid_confirmation_state":
152:      return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_CONFIRMATION_INVALID");
153:  }
154:}
155:
156:function mapProviderResult(result: ResendEmailResult): EmailDeliveryResult {
157:  if (result.status === "accepted") {
158:    return {
159:      status: "accepted",
160:      providerMessageId: result.providerMessageId,
161:    };
162:  }
163:
164:  if (result.status === "misconfigured") {
165:    return temporaryFailure("RESEND_NOT_CONFIGURED");
166:  }
167:
168:  if (result.status === "invalid_input") {
169:    return permanentFailure("RESEND_INVALID_INPUT");
170:  }
171:
172:  return {
173:    status: result.status,
174:    errorCode: result.errorCode,
175:  };
176:}
177:
178:export function createCourseLessonEmailDelivery(
179:  env: CourseLessonEmailDeliveryEnv,
180:  dependencies: CourseLessonEmailDeliveryDependencies = defaultDependencies,
181:): EmailDeliveryAdapter {
182:  return {
183:    async deliver(input: EmailDeliveryInput): Promise<EmailDeliveryResult> {
184:      if (!isCourseLessonJobType(input.jobType)) {
185:        return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_JOB_TYPE_INVALID");
186:      }
187:
188:      let prepared: PrepareCourseLessonDeliveryResult;
189:
190:      try {
191:        prepared = await dependencies.prepareCourseLessonDelivery(
192:          env,
193:          input.jobId,
194:        );
195:      } catch {
196:        return temporaryFailure("COURSE_LESSON_PREPARATION_EXCEPTION");
197:      }
198:
199:      if (prepared.status !== "ready") {
200:        return mapPreparationFailure(prepared);
201:      }
202:
203:      const getContentStatus =
204:        dependencies.getCourseLessonContentStatus ??
205:        getDefaultCourseLessonContentStatus;
206:
207:      const contentStatus = getContentStatus(prepared.courseSlug);
208:
209:      if (contentStatus === "unsupported") {
210:        return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_CONTENT_UNSUPPORTED");
211:      }
212:
213:      if (contentStatus !== "approved") {
214:        return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_CONTENT_NOT_APPROVED");
215:      }
216:
217:      if (dependencies.enableProviderDelivery !== true) {
218:        return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_CONTENT_NOT_ENABLED");
219:      }
220:
221:      const compose =
222:        dependencies.composeCourseLessonEmail ?? composeCourseLessonEmail;
223:
224:      let content: ComposedCourseLessonEmail;
225:
226:      try {
227:        content = compose({
228:          courseSlug: prepared.courseSlug,
229:          jobType: input.jobType,
230:          firstName: prepared.firstName,
231:          micrositeBaseUrl: SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_ORIGIN,
232:          mainSiteBaseUrl: PIPSTART_EMAIL_ORIGIN,
233:        });
234:      } catch {
235:        return permanentFailure("COURSE_LESSON_CONTENT_COMPOSITION_INVALID");
236:      }
237:
238:      const send =
239:        dependencies.sendEmailWithResend ?? sendCourseLessonWithResend;
240:
241:      let provider: ResendEmailResult;
242:
243:      try {
244:        provider = await send(env, {
245:          to: prepared.recipientEmail,
246:          subject: content.subject,
247:          html: content.html,
248:          text: content.text,
249:          idempotencyKey: input.idempotencyKey,
250:        });
251:      } catch {
252:        return temporaryFailure("RESEND_DELIVERY_EXCEPTION");
253:      }
254:
255:      return mapProviderResult(provider);
256:    },
257:  };
258:}

22:>;

36:  );

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\email-queue.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:export const SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION = 1 as const;
2:
3:export const SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPES = [
4:  "course_confirmation",
5:  "course_day_1",
6:  "course_day_2",
7:  "course_day_3",
8:  "course_day_4",
9:  "course_day_5",
10:] as const;
11:
12:export type SkillcimaEmailQueueJobType =
13:  (typeof SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPES)[number];
14:
15:const SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPE_SET: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(
16:  SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPES,
17:);
18:
19:export function isSkillcimaEmailQueueJobType(
20:  value: unknown,
21:): value is SkillcimaEmailQueueJobType {
22:  return (
23:    typeof value === "string" && SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPE_SET.has(value)
24:  );
25:}
26:
27:export interface SkillcimaEmailQueueMessage {
28:  version: typeof SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
29:  jobId: string;
30:  jobType: SkillcimaEmailQueueJobType;
31:}
32:
33:export interface EmailQueueBinding {
34:  send(message: SkillcimaEmailQueueMessage): Promise<void>;
35:}
36:
37:export function createEmailQueueMessage(input: {
38:  jobId: string;
39:  jobType: SkillcimaEmailQueueJobType;
40:}): SkillcimaEmailQueueMessage {
41:  return {
42:    version: SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION,
43:    jobId: input.jobId,
44:    jobType: input.jobType,
45:  };
46:}

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\email-queue-job-types.test.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
2:
3:import {
4:  createEmailQueueMessage,
5:  isSkillcimaEmailQueueJobType,
6:  SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPES,
7:  SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION,
8:} from "./email-queue";
9:import { parseQueueMessage } from "./email-queue-processor";
10:
11:const jobId = "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111";
12:
13:describe("Skillcima five-day email Queue job-type contract", () => {
14:  it.each(SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_JOB_TYPES)(
15:    "creates and parses %s without changing the Queue envelope",
16:    (jobType) => {
17:      const message = createEmailQueueMessage({
18:        jobId,
19:        jobType,
20:      });
21:
22:      expect(message).toEqual({
23:        version: SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION,
24:        jobId,
25:        jobType,
26:      });
27:
28:      expect(Object.keys(message).sort()).toEqual([
29:        "jobId",
30:        "jobType",
31:        "version",
32:      ]);
33:
34:      expect(isSkillcimaEmailQueueJobType(jobType)).toBe(true);
35:
36:      expect(parseQueueMessage(message)).toEqual(message);
37:    },
38:  );
39:
40:  it("rejects unsupported email workflow types at runtime", () => {
41:    expect(isSkillcimaEmailQueueJobType("unsupported_email_workflow")).toBe(
42:      false,
43:    );
44:
45:    expect(
46:      parseQueueMessage({
47:        version: SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION,
48:        jobId,
49:        jobType: "unsupported_email_workflow",
50:      }),
51:    ).toBeNull();
52:  });
53:
54:  it("does not weaken version, UUID, or exact-envelope validation", () => {
55:    expect(
56:      parseQueueMessage({
57:        version: 99,
58:        jobId,
59:        jobType: "course_day_1",
60:      }),
61:    ).toBeNull();
62:
63:    expect(
64:      parseQueueMessage({
65:        version: SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION,
66:        jobId: "not-a-uuid",
67:        jobType: "course_day_1",
68:      }),
69:    ).toBeNull();
70:
71:    expect(
72:      parseQueueMessage({
73:        version: SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_MESSAGE_VERSION,
74:        jobId,
75:        jobType: "course_day_1",
76:        email: "must-not-be-in-queue@example.com",
77:      }),
78:    ).toBeNull();
79:  });
80:});

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\fingerprint.ts

1:import type { LeadRequestData } from "@repo/validation";
2:
3:interface FingerprintPayload {
4:  lead: {
5:    firstName: string | null;
6:    email: string;
7:    privacyAcknowledged: boolean;
8:    newsletterConsent: boolean;
9:  };
10:}
11:
12:function bytesToHex(bytes: ArrayBuffer): string {
13:  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(bytes))
14:    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
15:    .join("");
16:}
17:
18:export function createFingerprintPayload(
19:  request: LeadRequestData,
20:): FingerprintPayload {
21:  return {
22:    lead: {
23:      firstName: request.lead.firstName ?? null,
24:      email: request.lead.email,
25:      privacyAcknowledged: request.lead.privacyAcknowledged,
26:      newsletterConsent: request.lead.newsletterConsent,
27:    },
28:  };
29:}
30:
31:export async function createLeadRequestFingerprint(
32:  request: LeadRequestData,
33:): Promise<string> {
34:  const payload = createFingerprintPayload(request);
35:
36:  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(payload));
37:
38:  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoded);
39:
40:  return bytesToHex(digest);
41:}

54:interface ApiError {
55:  code: string;
56:  message: string;
57:}

59:interface ApiSuccess<T> {
60:  ok: true;
61:  data: T;

71:function isLocalHostname(hostname: string): boolean {
72:  return hostname === "localhost" || hostname === "127.0.0.1";
73:}
74:
75:async function createEmailRateLimitKey(email: string): Promise<string> {
76:  const encodedEmail = new TextEncoder().encode(email);
77:
78:  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encodedEmail);
79:
80:  const hash = Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
81:    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
82:    .join("");
83:
84:  return `skillcima:lead:${hash}`;
85:}
86:
87:function getAllowedOrigin(request: Request): string | null {
88:  const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");
89:
90:  if (!origin) {
91:    return null;
92:  }
93:
94:  const requestUrl = new URL(request.url);
95:
96:  if (isLocalHostname(requestUrl.hostname)) {
97:    return origin === requestUrl.origin ? origin : null;
98:  }
99:
100:  return PRODUCTION_ORIGINS.has(origin) ? origin : null;
101:}
102:
103:function createHeaders(origin?: string): Headers {
104:  const headers = new Headers({
105:    "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
106:    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
107:    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
108:  });
109:
110:  if (origin) {
111:    headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin);
112:    headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "POST, OPTIONS");
113:    headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type");
114:    headers.set("Vary", "Origin");
115:  }
116:
117:  return headers;
118:}
119:
120:function successResponse<T>(
121:  requestId: string,
122:  data: T,
123:  status = 200,
124:  origin?: string,
125:): Response {
126:  const body: ApiSuccess<T> = {
127:    ok: true,
128:    data,
129:    requestId,
130:  };
131:
132:  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
133:    status,
134:    headers: createHeaders(origin),
135:  });
136:}
137:
138:function errorResponse(
139:  requestId: string,
140:  status: number,
141:  error: ApiError,
142:  origin?: string,
143:): Response {
144:  const body: ApiFailure = {
145:    ok: false,
146:    error,
147:    requestId,
148:  };
149:
150:  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
151:    status,
152:    headers: createHeaders(origin),
153:  });
154:}
155:
156:async function readBodyWithLimit(
157:  request: Request,
158:  maxBytes: number,
159:): Promise<string> {
160:  const contentLength = request.headers.get("Content-Length");
161:
162:  if (contentLength) {
163:    const declaredLength = Number(contentLength);
164:
165:    if (Number.isFinite(declaredLength) && declaredLength > maxBytes) {
166:      throw new Error("REQUEST_BODY_TOO_LARGE");
167:    }
168:  }
169:
170:  if (!request.body) {
171:    return "";
172:  }
173:
174:  const reader = request.body.getReader();
175:  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
176:
177:  let totalBytes = 0;
178:  let body = "";
179:
180:  while (true) {
181:    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
182:
183:    if (done) {
184:      break;
185:    }
186:
187:    totalBytes += value.byteLength;
188:
189:    if (totalBytes > maxBytes) {
190:      await reader.cancel();
191:      throw new Error("REQUEST_BODY_TOO_LARGE");
192:    }
193:
194:    body += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
195:  }
196:
197:  body += decoder.decode();
198:
199:  return body;
200:}
201:
202:async function handleLeadRequest(
203:  request: Request,
204:  requestId: string,
205:  env: Env,
206:): Promise<Response> {
207:  const allowedOrigin = getAllowedOrigin(request);
208:
209:  if (!allowedOrigin) {
210:    return errorResponse(requestId, 403, {
211:      code: "ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED",
212:      message: "This request origin is not allowed.",
213:    });
214:  }
215:
216:  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
217:    return new Response(null, {
218:      status: 204,
219:      headers: createHeaders(allowedOrigin),
220:    });
221:  }
222:
223:  if (request.method !== "POST") {
224:    return errorResponse(
225:      requestId,
226:      405,
227:      {
228:        code: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED",
229:        message: "This endpoint only accepts POST requests.",
230:      },
231:      allowedOrigin,
232:    );
233:  }
234:
235:  const contentType = request.headers
236:    .get("Content-Type")
237:    ?.split(";")[0]
238:    ?.trim()
239:    .toLowerCase();
240:
241:  if (contentType !== "application/json") {
242:    return errorResponse(
243:      requestId,
244:      415,
245:      {
246:        code: "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE",
247:        message: "Content-Type must be application/json.",
248:      },
249:      allowedOrigin,
250:    );
251:  }
252:
253:  let rawBody: string;
254:
255:  try {
256:    rawBody = await readBodyWithLimit(request, MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES);
257:  } catch (error) {
258:    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "REQUEST_BODY_TOO_LARGE") {
259:      return errorResponse(
260:        requestId,
261:        413,
262:        {
263:          code: "REQUEST_BODY_TOO_LARGE",
264:          message: "The request body exceeds the maximum allowed size.",
265:        },
266:        allowedOrigin,
267:      );
268:    }
269:
270:    return errorResponse(
271:      requestId,
272:      400,
273:      {
274:        code: "REQUEST_BODY_READ_FAILED",
275:        message: "The request body could not be read.",
276:      },
277:      allowedOrigin,
278:    );
279:  }
280:
281:  if (!rawBody.trim()) {
282:    return errorResponse(
283:      requestId,
284:      400,
285:      {
286:        code: "EMPTY_REQUEST_BODY",
287:        message: "A JSON request body is required.",
288:      },
289:      allowedOrigin,
290:    );
291:  }
292:
293:  let parsedBody: unknown;
294:
295:  try {
296:    parsedBody = JSON.parse(rawBody);
297:  } catch {
298:    return errorResponse(
299:      requestId,
300:      400,
301:      {
302:        code: "INVALID_JSON",
303:        message: "The request body contains invalid JSON.",
304:      },
305:      allowedOrigin,
306:    );
307:  }
308:
309:  const validationResult = leadRequestSchema.safeParse(parsedBody);
310:
311:  if (!validationResult.success) {
312:    return new Response(
313:      JSON.stringify({
314:        ok: false,
315:        error: {
316:          code: "VALIDATION_FAILED",
317:          message: "The submitted data is invalid.",
318:          issues: validationResult.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
319:            path: issue.path.join("."),
320:            message: issue.message,
321:            code: issue.code,
322:          })),
323:        },
324:        requestId,
325:      }),
326:      {
327:        status: 400,
328:        headers: createHeaders(allowedOrigin),
329:      },
330:    );
331:  }
332:
333:  const validatedRequest = validationResult.data;
334:
335:  const rateLimitKey = await createEmailRateLimitKey(
336:    validatedRequest.lead.email,
337:  );
338:
339:  const rateLimitResult = await env.LEAD_RATE_LIMITER.limit({
340:    key: rateLimitKey,
341:  });
342:
343:  if (!rateLimitResult.success) {
344:    console.warn(
345:      JSON.stringify({
346:        requestId,
347:        route: "/api/v1/lead",
348:        stage: "rate_limit",
349:        result: "blocked",
350:      }),
351:    );
352:
353:    const response = errorResponse(
354:      requestId,
355:      429,
356:      {
357:        code: "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
358:        message: "Too many attempts. Please wait before trying again.",
359:      },
360:      allowedOrigin,
361:    );
362:
363:    response.headers.set("Retry-After", "60");
364:
365:    return response;
366:  }
367:
368:  if (!env.TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY) {
369:    return errorResponse(
370:      requestId,
371:      503,
372:      {
373:        code: "TURNSTILE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
374:        message: "Verification is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
375:      },
376:      allowedOrigin,
377:    );
378:  }
379:
380:  const remoteIp = request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") ?? undefined;
381:
382:  const turnstileResult = await verifyTurnstile({
383:    secret: env.TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY,
384:    token: validatedRequest.turnstileToken,
385:    remoteIp,
386:    idempotencyKey: validatedRequest.submissionId,
387:  });
388:
389:  if (turnstileResult.status === "unavailable") {
390:    return errorResponse(
391:      requestId,
392:      503,
393:      {
394:        code: "TURNSTILE_UNAVAILABLE",
395:        message: "Verification is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
396:      },
397:      allowedOrigin,
398:    );
399:  }
400:
401:  if (turnstileResult.status === "failed") {
402:    return errorResponse(
403:      requestId,
404:      403,
405:      {
406:        code: "TURNSTILE_FAILED",
407:        message: "Verification failed. Please try again.",
408:      },
409:      allowedOrigin,
410:    );
411:  }
412:
413:  const persistenceResult = await persistVerifiedLead(env, validatedRequest);
414:
415:  if (persistenceResult.status === "conflict") {
416:    return errorResponse(
417:      requestId,
418:      409,
419:      {
420:        code: "SUBMISSION_CONFLICT",
421:        message:
422:          "This submission ID has already been used with different data.",
423:      },
424:      allowedOrigin,
425:    );
426:  }
427:
428:  if (persistenceResult.status === "misconfigured") {
429:    return errorResponse(
430:      requestId,
431:      503,
432:      {
433:        code: "DATABASE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
434:        message:
435:          "The submission service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
436:      },
437:      allowedOrigin,
438:    );
439:  }
440:
441:  if (persistenceResult.status === "unavailable") {
442:    console.error(
443:      JSON.stringify({
444:        requestId,
445:        route: "/api/v1/lead",
446:        stage: "persistence",
447:        result: "unavailable",
448:        httpStatus: persistenceResult.httpStatus ?? null,
449:      }),
450:    );
451:
452:    return errorResponse(
453:      requestId,
454:      503,
455:      {
456:        code: "DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE",
457:        message:
458:          "The submission service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
459:      },
460:      allowedOrigin,
461:    );
462:  }
463:
464:  return successResponse(
465:    requestId,
466:    {
467:      status: "completed",
468:      submissionId: validatedRequest.submissionId,
469:      leadId: persistenceResult.leadId,
470:      enrolmentId: persistenceResult.enrolmentId,
471:      replayed: persistenceResult.replayed,
472:    },
473:    200,
474:    allowedOrigin,
475:  );
476:}
477:
478:const worker = {
479:  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
480:    const requestId = crypto.randomUUID();
481:    const url = new URL(request.url);
482:
483:    if (url.pathname === "/api/v1/health") {
484:      if (request.method !== "GET") {
485:        return errorResponse(requestId, 405, {
486:          code: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED",
487:          message: "This endpoint only accepts GET requests.",
488:        });
489:      }
490:
491:      const database = await checkSupabaseConnection(env);
492:
493:      if (database.status === "misconfigured") {
494:        return errorResponse(requestId, 503, {
495:          code: "DATABASE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
496:          message: "The database connection is not configured.",
497:        });
498:      }
499:
500:      if (database.status === "unavailable") {
501:        console.error(
502:          JSON.stringify({
503:            requestId,
504:            event: "health_check_failed",
505:            dependency: "supabase",
506:            httpStatus: database.httpStatus ?? null,
507:          }),
508:        );
509:
510:        return errorResponse(requestId, 503, {
511:          code: "DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE",
512:          message: "The database service is currently unavailable.",
513:        });
514:      }
515:
516:      return successResponse(requestId, {
517:        status: "ok",
518:        service: "skillcima-api",
519:        version: "1",
520:        database: "ok",
521:      });
522:    }
523:
524:    if (url.pathname === "/api/v1/lead") {
525:      return handleLeadRequest(request, requestId, env);
526:    }
527:
528:    if (url.pathname === "/api/v1/confirm") {
529:      return handleCourseConfirmationRequest(request, env);
530:    }
531:
532:    if (url.pathname === "/api/v1/unsubscribe") {
533:      return handleNewsletterUnsubscribeRequest(request, env);
534:    }
535:
536:    if (url.pathname === "/api/v1/webhooks/resend") {
537:      return handleResendWebhookRequest(request, env);
538:    }
539:
540:    return errorResponse(requestId, 404, {
541:      code: "API_ROUTE_NOT_FOUND",
542:      message: "The requested API route does not exist.",
543:    });
544:  },
545:
546:  async queue(batch: EmailQueueRuntimeNamedBatch, env: Env): Promise<void> {
547:    if (batch.queue === SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_DLQ_NAME) {
548:      const result = await processEmailDeadLetterBatch(env, batch);
549:
550:      const log = result.retried > 0 ? console.error : console.log;
551:
552:      log(
553:        JSON.stringify({
554:          event: "email_queue_dlq_batch_completed",
555:          queue: batch.queue,
556:          received: result.received,
557:          acknowledged: result.acknowledged,
558:          retried: result.retried,
559:          invalidMessages: result.invalidMessages,
560:          reconciled: result.reconciled,
561:          terminalNoops: result.terminalNoops,
562:          stateFailures: result.stateFailures,
563:        }),
564:      );
565:
566:      return;
567:    }
568:
569:    if (batch.queue !== SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE_NAME) {
570:      for (const message of batch.messages) {
571:        message.retry({
572:          delaySeconds: EMAIL_QUEUE_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS,
573:        });
574:      }
575:
576:      console.error(
577:        JSON.stringify({
578:          event: "email_queue_unknown_batch",
579:          queue: batch.queue,
580:          received: batch.messages.length,
581:          retried: batch.messages.length,
582:        }),
583:      );
584:
585:      return;
586:    }
587:
588:    const delivery = createEmailDeliveryRouter({
589:      confirmationDelivery: createConfirmationEmailDelivery(env),
590:      courseLessonDelivery: createCourseLessonEmailDelivery(env, {
591:        prepareCourseLessonDelivery,
592:        enableProviderDelivery:
593:          env.SKILLCIMA_COURSE_LESSON_PROVIDER_ENABLED === "true",
594:      }),
595:    });
596:
597:    const result = await processEmailQueueBatch(env, batch, delivery);
598:
599:    const hasRetries = result.retried > 0 || result.processorExceptions > 0;
600:
601:    const log = hasRetries ? console.error : console.log;
602:
603:    log(
604:      JSON.stringify({
605:        event: "email_queue_batch_completed",
606:        queue: batch.queue,
607:        deliveryMode: "email_router",
608:        received: result.received,
609:        acknowledged: result.acknowledged,
610:        retried: result.retried,
611:        processorExceptions: result.processorExceptions,
612:      }),
613:    );
614:  },
615:
616:  async scheduled(_controller: unknown, env: Env): Promise<void> {
617:    const result = await dispatchEmailOutbox(env, env.SKILLCIMA_EMAIL_QUEUE);
618:
619:    if (result.status !== "completed") {
620:      console.error(
621:        JSON.stringify({
622:          event: "email_outbox_dispatch_failed",
623:          status: result.status,
624:          stage: result.status === "unavailable" ? result.stage : null,
625:          httpStatus:
626:            result.status === "unavailable"
627:              ? (result.httpStatus ?? null)
628:              : null,
629:        }),
630:      );
631:
632:      return;
633:    }
634:
635:    const hasFailures =
636:      result.queueFailures > 0 || result.stateUpdateFailures > 0;
637:
638:    const log = hasFailures ? console.error : console.log;
639:
640:    log(
641:      JSON.stringify({
642:        event: "email_outbox_dispatch_completed",
643:        selected: result.selected,
644:        sent: result.sent,
645:        markedQueued: result.markedQueued,
646:        queueFailures: result.queueFailures,
647:        stateUpdateFailures: result.stateUpdateFailures,
648:      }),
649:    );
650:  },
651:};
652:
653:export default worker;

FILE: D:\Website-Projects\pipstart-platform\apps\skillcima\worker\lead-persistence.ts
------------------------------------------------------------
1:import type { PendingConsentEvent } from "./consent";
2:import type { SupabaseEnv } from "./supabase";
3:
4:export const SKILLCIMA_COURSE_SLUG = "forex-foundations";
5:
6:export interface CompleteLeadSubmissionInput {
7:  submissionId: string;
8:  requestFingerprint: string;
9:  firstName?: string;
10:  email: string;
11:  consentEvents: PendingConsentEvent[];
12:}
13:
14:export type CompleteLeadSubmissionResult =
15:  | {
16:      status: "completed";
17:      leadId: string;
18:      enrolmentId: string;
19:      replayed: boolean;
20:    }
21:  | {
22:      status: "conflict";
23:    }
24:  | {
25:      status: "misconfigured";
26:    }
27:  | {
28:      status: "unavailable";
29:      httpStatus?: number;
30:    };
31:
32:interface RpcRow {
33:  result_lead_id: string;
34:  result_enrolment_id: string;
35:  result_status: string;
36:  result_replayed: boolean;
37:}
38:
39:interface RpcErrorBody {
40:  code?: string;
41:  message?: string;
42:}
43:
44:interface SupabaseConfiguration {
45:  url: string;
46:  secretKey: string;
47:}
48:
49:function getSupabaseConfiguration(
50:  env: SupabaseEnv,
51:): SupabaseConfiguration | null {
52:  const url = env.SUPABASE_URL?.trim();
53:  const secretKey = env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY?.trim();
54:
55:  if (!url || !secretKey) {
56:    return null;
57:  }
58:
59:  return {
60:    url: url.replace(/\/+$/, ""),
61:    secretKey,
62:  };
63:}
64:
65:function toRpcConsentEvents(
66:  events: PendingConsentEvent[],
67:): Record<string, string>[] {
68:  return events.map((event) => ({
69:    category: event.category,
70:    action: event.action,
71:    privacy_notice_version: event.privacyNoticeVersion,
72:    consent_wording: event.consentWording,
73:    consent_wording_version: event.consentWordingVersion,
74:    landing_page_version: event.landingPageVersion,
75:  }));
76:}
77:
78:async function readRpcError(response: Response): Promise<RpcErrorBody | null> {
79:  try {
80:    return (await response.json()) as RpcErrorBody;
81:  } catch {
82:    return null;
83:  }
84:}
85:
86:export async function completeLeadSubmission(
87:  env: SupabaseEnv,
88:  input: CompleteLeadSubmissionInput,
89:): Promise<CompleteLeadSubmissionResult> {
90:  const configuration = getSupabaseConfiguration(env);
91:
92:  if (!configuration) {
93:    return {
94:      status: "misconfigured",
95:    };
96:  }
97:
98:  const url = new URL(
99:    `${configuration.url}/rest/v1/rpc/skillcima_complete_lead_submission`,
100:  );
101:
102:  try {
103:    const response = await fetch(url, {
104:      method: "POST",
105:      headers: {
106:        Accept: "application/json",
107:        "Content-Type": "application/json",
108:        apikey: configuration.secretKey,
109:      },
110:      body: JSON.stringify({
111:        p_submission_id: input.submissionId,
112:        p_request_fingerprint: input.requestFingerprint,
113:        p_first_name: input.firstName ?? null,
114:        p_email: input.email,
115:        p_course_slug: SKILLCIMA_COURSE_SLUG,
116:        p_consent_events: toRpcConsentEvents(input.consentEvents),
117:      }),
118:    });
119:
120:    if (!response.ok) {
121:      const httpStatus = response.status;
122:      const error = await readRpcError(response);
123:
124:      if (error?.message === "SUBMISSION_CONFLICT") {
125:        return {
126:          status: "conflict",
127:        };
128:      }
129:
130:      return {
131:        status: "unavailable",
132:        httpStatus,
133:      };
134:    }
135:
136:    const rows = (await response.json()) as RpcRow[];
137:    const row = rows[0];
138:
139:    if (
140:      rows.length !== 1 ||
141:      !row ||
142:      row.result_status !== "completed" ||
143:      typeof row.result_lead_id !== "string" ||
144:      typeof row.result_enrolment_id !== "string" ||
145:      typeof row.result_replayed !== "boolean"
146:    ) {
147:      return {
148:        status: "unavailable",
149:        httpStatus: response.status,
150:      };
151:    }
152:
153:    return {
154:      status: "completed",
155:      leadId: row.result_lead_id,
156:      enrolmentId: row.result_enrolment_id,
157:      replayed: row.result_replayed,
158:    };
159:  } catch {
160:    return {
161:      status: "unavailable",
162:    };
163:  }
164:}

11:} from "./newsletter-unsubscribe-route";
12:
13:import type {
14:  WithdrawNewsletterByTokenResult,
15:} from "./newsletter-unsubscribe-state";
16:
17:import type { SupabaseEnv } from "./supabase";
18:
19:const token = "a".repeat(64);
20:
21:const leadId =
22:  "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111";
23:
24:const enrolmentId =
25:  "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222";
26:
27:const grantId =
28:  "33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333";
29:
30:const withdrawalId =
31:  "44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444";
32:
33:const env: SupabaseEnv = {
34:  SUPABASE_URL:
35:    "https://example.supabase.co",
36:
37:  SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY:
38:    "test-secret",
39:};
40:
41:function createRequest(
42:  body: unknown,
43:  options: {
44:    method?: string;
45:    contentType?: string;
46:  } = {},
47:): Request {
48:  const method =
49:    options.method ?? "POST";
50:
51:  return new Request(
52:    "https://skillcima.com/api/v1/unsubscribe",
53:    {
54:      method,
55:
56:      headers:
57:        method === "POST"
58:          ? {
59:              "Content-Type":
60:                options.contentType ??
61:                "application/json",
62:            }
63:          : undefined,
64:
65:      body:
66:        method === "POST"
67:          ? typeof body === "string"
68:            ? body
69:            : JSON.stringify(body)
70:          : undefined,
71:    },
72:  );
73:}
74:
75:function createDependencies(
76:  result:
77:    WithdrawNewsletterByTokenResult,
78:): NewsletterUnsubscribeRouteDependencies {
79:  return {
80:    withdrawNewsletterByToken:
81:      vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(result),
82:  };
83:}

366:          createDependencies({
367:            status: "not_found",
368:          });
369:
370:        const response =
371:          await handleNewsletterUnsubscribeRequest(
372:            createRequest({
373:              token:
374:                "a".repeat(3000),
375:            }),
376:            env,
377:            dependencies,
378:          );
379:
380:        expect(response.status).toBe(
381:          413,
382:        );
383:

385:          dependencies.withdrawNewsletterByToken,

387:      },
388:    );

400:              status:
401:                "invalid_consent_state",
402:              leadId,
403:              enrolmentId,
404:              grantConsentEventId:
405:                grantId,
406:              latestConsentEventId:
407:                withdrawalId,
408:            }),
409:          );
410:
411:        expect(response.status).toBe(
412:          409,
413:        );
414:
415:        await expect(
416:          response.json(),
417:        ).resolves.toMatchObject({
418:          error: {
419:            code:
420:              "UNSUBSCRIBE_STATE_INVALID",
421:          },
422:        });
423:      },
424:    );
425:
